# Expression K correction 03

`source-candidate-03.json` binds the preserved full40 candidate and the three repairs below. The prior GPT-6 and Grok reviews are retained unchanged under `deliverables/sp03-expression-k-2026-09-10/`; their `REQUEST_CHANGES` verdicts do not approve this changed candidate.

1. `exInput` now applies the 65536-byte schema limit before J-domain validation, matching `createExpressionContractV1`. The fixture generator adds `invalid-schema-domain-65536` (invalid J domain, expected `INPUT_SCHEMA`) and `invalid-schema-domain-65537` (same domain, expected `INPUT_BOUND`).
2. `command()` enforces the declared post-capture limit on `stdout` plus `stderr`, including an exact-boundary control and a one-byte-over control. It retains both captured files.
3. Semantic expression artifacts remain J-canonical. Only `*.command.json` telemetry uses ordinary JSON, so actual float `elapsedSeconds` and `aggregateSeconds` are retained and an already-counted attempt is never reset.

The candidate has 115 fixtures. `resource-proposal-03.json` requests, but does not consume, a reviewed delta of two krun calls and forty seconds: 1 compile, 115 traces, 2552 seconds total. It binds only `/home/charl/Moriarty/.worktrees/roadmap-loop-prompt`; the preserved original worktree remains untouched.

Offline validation before the repairs was red: the runner failed with `EXPRESSION_TRANSPORT`, the aggregate-limit test had no limit table, and the K-order assertion failed. After the repairs, Python runner/source tests pass (9 tests) and the published TypeScript fixture comparison passes all 115 cases. Parent separately observed real harmless subprocess telemetry at the 64 MiB aggregate boundary, one byte above it, and stderr-only overflow; no K/native command ran.

Remaining obligations: fresh Astra medium and Grok 4.6 high exact source/resource reviews, root preflight/admission, then at most the separately admitted bounded K command and fresh result reviews. This document makes no K correspondence, compile, proof, financial, ledger, network, or publication claim.

=== plugins/moriarty-dev/skills/develop/SKILL.md sha256 fea833422adcacc32915b67edd2a7ec8efa24b1407a7366296b1829852c12250 ===
---
name: develop
description: Moriarty development workflow enforcing stop rules, eligible next-action selection, and bounded guarded dispatch.
---

# Moriarty Development Workflow

Use this skill to guide and guard development on the Moriarty financial language for Midnight.
It enforces repository stop rules, prevents orchestration displacement, and directs execution toward concrete, demonstrable product capabilities.

## Operating Principles

1. **User Steering**: User requests, intent, or interruptions always take precedence over automatic action selection.
2. **Product Routing**:
   - Current plugin implementation: GPT-6, as selected by the user. Follow later user routing for subsequent product work.
   - Independent result review: separate, fresh GPT-6 Astra and Grok 4.6 high reviewers check the exact candidate bytes, per the September 9 user routing. Preserve historical verdict identities; both current audits are required.
   - Consequential design decisions retain majority rule (two substantive agreeing votes). Routine repairs within approved scope proceed without repeated design votes.
3. **Guarded Dispatch**: Launch registered campaign actions through the plugin CLI `run`, which validates authority, candidate commitments, debit identity and stop rules in an atomic reservation. Routine authorized file inspection and source edits do not need a new runner, campaign or design vote.
4. **Host Interception and Fallback**: Direct CLI execution (`cli.py run`) is the verified fallback procedure whenever host hook trust is unverified, degraded, or wrapper-only.
5. **Transaction Transparency**: When Midnight public transaction notifications are present, always post pending transaction IDs in the conversation before providing a network status summary.
6. **No Synthetic Progress**: Tests and packet approvals cannot establish missing behavior. Mandatory Preview financial settlement and PCD correspondence remain open until proven by real ledger evidence.

## Standard Product Development Workflow

The required sequence for all Moriarty tasks:
```text
User Intent -> status -> next -> GPT-6 Implementation -> Actual Checks -> Separate GPT-6 Review
```

### 1. Inspect Status (`status`)
Check current capability, last demonstrated result, active stop rules, and pending transactions:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . status
```
If pending transaction IDs are returned, obtain their current statuses from `report --json` and post a dedicated conversation message containing only the public notification lines (one or more), before a separate network status summary:

```text
Midnight Preview transaction <TX_ID>: <STATUS>.
```

Use `notify --tx-id <TX_ID> --status submitted` after selecting a real public submission observation, and the same command with `unknown-finality`, `failed` or `confirmed` for subsequent observed changes. These are reported observations; the command does not query Midnight or establish financial acceptance. Never use test IDs as real transaction evidence.

After the line has been emitted in this conversation, call `deliver --tx-id <TX_ID>`. The CLI checks the current Codex session's emitted assistant message and stores its identity and digest. Tool output and caller-written acknowledgement JSON do not establish delivery. If the host record is unavailable, leave the notification pending. A later chain status becomes pending again. Delivery means an emitted host message, not proof that the user read it.

### 2. Determine Next Eligible Action (`next`)
Query policy for the next authorized action:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . next
```
If an implementation attempt is blocked by repeated failures (two cycles of the same defect class), the policy enforces reproduction (`reproduce`) before any retry.

### 3. Implement Focused Repair (GPT-6)
Implement the required repair within approved scope and limits without widening the design or erasing prior failures.

### 4. Execute and Check Action (`run`)
Run the action through the guarded CLI:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . run --action <ACTION_ID>
```
Exit codes:
- `0`: Success.
- `2`: Policy denial (repeated failure, administrative displacement, or resource exhaustion).
- `3`: Unavailable or stale inputs.
- `4`: Child command failure.

### 5. Ingest Independent Review (`review`)
When fresh GPT-6 Astra review completes on the exact candidate bytes, ingest the receipt:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . review --receipt <PATH_TO_RECEIPT_JSON>
```
Author self-review, mismatched candidate hashes, and empty scopes are rejected.

### 6. Report Progress Honestly (`report`)
Generate an all-sprint status report derived from actual program records:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . report
```

### 7. Inspect Environment Health (`doctor`)
Check repository store state, hook registration, and host trust:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . doctor
```
Reports `unverified` or `installed-unverified`. Hook definition and cached installation paths do not establish runtime trust or interception. Use the guarded CLI and retain a separate actual-host smoke result for each tested tool path.

Do not expand orchestration infrastructure to follow this workflow. Name the next demonstrable capability, implement its smallest authorized repair, and check its behavior. Preserve unresolved safety stops and independent acceptance gates.

=== experiments/moriarty-language/formal/k/expression-infer.k sha256 7d0540650704292bf4b1b36e3f065fb08815a35e69fdc9d446978f7de50f687c ===
requires "expression-shape.k"
requires "expression-values.k"
module MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-INFER
  imports MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-SHAPE
  imports MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-VALUES
  syntax EXInference ::= exTyped(EJSON) | exTypedList(EJList) | exTypeError(String,EJSON,EJList)
  syntax EXInference ::= exInfer(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,Bool,EJList) [function, total]
                       | exInferStart(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,Bool,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                       | exInferChecked(String,String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                       | exInferChildren(EJList,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,EJList,Int) [function, total]
                       | exTypePrepend(EXInference,EXInference) [function, total]
                       | exInferFinish(EXInference,String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                       | exTypeSite(EXInference,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
  syntax Bool ::= exStatement(String) [function, total]
  rule exStatement(K) => K ==String "Require" orBool K ==String "Let" orBool K ==String "NextWrite" orBool K ==String "Ensure" orBool K ==String "Emit"
  rule exInfer(N,S,L,W,E,A,P) => exInferName(ejGet(N,"constructor"),N,S,L,W,E,A,P)
  syntax EXInference ::= exInferName(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,Bool,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exInferName(ejString(K),N,S,L,W,E,A,P) => exInferStart(K,ejGet(N,"operands"),S,L,W,E,A,ejGet(N,"span"),P)
  rule exInferName(_,N,_,_,_,_,_,P) => exTypeError("TYPE_CONSTRUCTOR",ejGet(N,"span"),P) [owise]
  rule exInferStart(K,O,S,L,W,E,A,Span,P) => exInferChecked(exFirst(exCheck(exStatement(K) ==Bool A,"TYPE_STATEMENT_PLACEMENT"),exPreType(K,O,S,L,W)),K,O,S,L,W,E,Span,P)
  rule exInferChecked("",K,O,S,L,W,E,Span,P) => exInferFinish(exInferChildren(exChildren(K,O),S,L,W,E,P,0),K,O,S,L,E,Span,P)
  rule exInferChecked(C,_,_,_,_,_,_,Span,P) => exTypeError(C,Span,P) requires C =/=String ""
  rule exInferChildren(ejNil(),_,_,_,_,_,_) => exTypedList(ejNil())
  rule exInferChildren(ejCons(N,R),S,L,W,E,P,I) => exTypePrepend(exInfer(N,S,L,W,E,false,ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil()))),exInferChildren(R,S,L,W,E,P,I +Int 1))
  rule exTypePrepend(exTyped(T),exTypedList(L)) => exTypedList(ejCons(T,L))
  rule exTypePrepend(exTypeError(C,S,P),_) => exTypeError(C,S,P)
  rule exTypePrepend(exTyped(_),exTypeError(C,S,P)) => exTypeError(C,S,P)
  rule exInferFinish(exTypeError(C,S,P),_,_,_,_,_,_,_) => exTypeError(C,S,P)
  rule exInferFinish(exTypedList(T),K,O,S,L,E,Span,P) => exTypeSite(exPrimitiveType(K,O,S,L,E,ejArray(T)),Span,P)
  rule exTypeSite(exTyped(T),_,_) => exTyped(T)
  rule exTypeSite(exTypeError(C,_,_),S,P) => exTypeError(C,S,P)
  syntax EJList ::= exChildren(String,EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exRoleChildren(EXRoles,EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exUnwrapArray(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exFieldExpressions(EJList) [function, total]
  rule exChildren(K,O) => exRoleChildren(exRoles(K),O)
  rule exRoleChildren(exRolesNil(),_) => ejNil()
  rule exRoleChildren(exRolesCons(exRole(N,"expr"),R),O) => ejCons(ejGet(O,N),exRoleChildren(R,O))
  rule exRoleChildren(exRolesCons(exRole(N,"list"),R),O) => ejAppend(exUnwrapArray(ejGet(O,N)),exRoleChildren(R,O))
  rule exRoleChildren(exRolesCons(exRole(N,"fields"),R),O) => ejAppend(exFieldExpressions(exUnwrapArray(ejGet(O,N))),exRoleChildren(R,O))
  rule exRoleChildren(exRolesCons(exRole(_,K),R),O) => exRoleChildren(R,O) requires K =/=String "expr" andBool K =/=String "list" andBool K =/=String "fields"
  rule exUnwrapArray(ejArray(L)) => L
  rule exUnwrapArray(_) => ejNil() [owise]
  rule exFieldExpressions(ejNil()) => ejNil()
  rule exFieldExpressions(ejCons(F,L)) => ejCons(ejGet(F,"value"),exFieldExpressions(L))
  syntax String ::= exPreType(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exBinder(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exBinderMaps(EJSON,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exRecordNames(EJSON,EJList,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exRecordCoverage(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exPreType("Let",O,S,L,_) => exBinder(ejGet(O,"name"),S,L)
  rule exPreType("NextWrite",O,S,_,W) => exWritePre(ejGet(O,"field"),S,W)
  syntax String ::= exWritePre(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exWritePre(ejString(F),S,W) => exFirst(exCheck(ejHas(ejGet(S,"fields"),F),"TYPE_NAME"),exFirst(exCheck(ejGet(ejGet(ejGet(S,"fields"),F),"writeClass") ==K ejString("ordinary"),"TYPE_FINANCIAL_WRITE"),exCheck(notBool ejHas(W,F),"TYPE_DUPLICATE_WRITE")))
  rule exWritePre(_,_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exPreType("Emit",O,S,_,_) => exCheck(exHasName(ejGet(S,"operations"),ejGet(O,"operation")),"TYPE_NAME")
  rule exPreType("ConstructRecord",O,S,_,_) => exFirst(exCheck(exHasName(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),ejGet(O,"recordType")),"TYPE_NAME"),exFirst(exRecordNames(O,exUnwrapArray(ejGet(O,"fields")),ejNil()),exRecordCoverage(exGetName(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),ejGet(O,"recordType")),exUnwrapArray(ejGet(O,"fields")))))
  rule exPreType(K,O,S,_,_) => exResolve(S,ejGet(O,"elementType"),"TYPE_LITERAL") requires K ==String "ConstructSome" orBool K ==String "ConstructNone"
  rule exPreType("ConstructCollection",O,S,_,_) => exFirst(exResolve(S,ejGet(O,"elementType"),"TYPE_LITERAL"),exCheck(exInt(ejGet(O,"capacity")) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(ejGet(O,"capacity")) <=Int 128 andBool exArrayLength(ejGet(O,"items")) <=Int exInt(ejGet(O,"capacity")),"TYPE_COLLECTION_BOUND"))
  rule exPreType(_,_,_,_,_) => "" [owise]
  syntax Bool ::= exHasName(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  syntax EJSON ::= exGetName(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exHasName(M,ejString(N)) => ejHas(M,N)
  rule exHasName(_,_) => false [owise]
  rule exGetName(M,ejString(N)) => ejGet(M,N)
  rule exGetName(_,_) => ejMissing() [owise]
  rule exBinder(ejString(N),S,L) => exFirst(exCheck(notBool ejHas(L,N) andBool N =/=String "pre" andBool N =/=String "post" andBool N =/=String "next","TYPE_DUPLICATE_BINDER"),exBinderMaps(ejString(N),S,exSchemaKeys()))
  rule exBinder(_,_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exBinderMaps(_,_,ejNil()) => ""
  rule exBinderMaps(N,S,ejCons(ejString(K),R)) => exFirst(exCheck(notBool exHasName(ejGet(S,K),N) andBool notBool exIn(N,ejGet(S,K)),"TYPE_DUPLICATE_BINDER"),exBinderMaps(N,S,R))
  rule exRecordNames(_,ejNil(),_) => ""
  rule exRecordNames(O,ejCons(F,R),Seen) => exFirst(exCheck(notBool ejMember(ejGet(F,"name"),Seen),"TYPE_DUPLICATE_FIELD"),exRecordNames(O,R,ejCons(ejGet(F,"name"),Seen)))
  rule exRecordCoverage(T,F) => exFirst(exCheck(exSize(T) ==Int ejLength(F),"TYPE_RECORD_FIELDS"),exRecordFieldNames(T,F))
  syntax String ::= exRecordFieldNames(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exRecordFieldNames(_,ejNil()) => ""
  rule exRecordFieldNames(T,ejCons(F,R)) => exFirst(exCheck(exHasName(T,ejGet(F,"name")),"TYPE_RECORD_FIELDS"),exRecordFieldNames(T,R))
  syntax EXInference ::= exPrimitiveType(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,Bool,EJSON) [function, total]
                       | exTypeIf(String,EJSON) [function, total]
                       | exLookupType(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exTypeIf("",T) => exTyped(T)
  rule exTypeIf(C,_) => exTypeError(C,exSynthetic(),ejNil()) requires C =/=String ""
  rule exLookupType(M,N) => exTypeIf(exCheck(exHasName(M,N),"TYPE_NAME"),exGetName(M,N))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ReadLocal",O,_,L,_,_) => exLookupType(L,ejGet(O,"name"))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ReadArg",O,S,_,_,_) => exLookupType(ejGet(S,"args"),ejGet(O,"name"))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ReadObs",O,S,_,_,_) => exLookupType(ejGet(S,"observations"),ejGet(O,"name"))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ReadPre",O,S,_,E,_) => exTypeIf(exFirst(exCheck(ejGet(O,"view") =/=K ejString("next"),"TYPE_NEXT_READ"),exFirst(exCheck(ejGet(O,"view") =/=K ejString("post") orBool E,"TYPE_POST_SCOPE"),exCheck(exHasName(ejGet(S,"fields"),ejGet(O,"field")),"TYPE_NAME"))),ejGet(exGetName(ejGet(S,"fields"),ejGet(O,"field")),"type"))
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,O,S,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exFirst(exCheck(ejAt(ejAt(T,0),0) ==K ejString("Record"),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exCheck(exHasName(exGetName(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),ejAt(ejAt(T,0),1)),ejGet(O,"field")),"TYPE_NAME")),exGetName(exGetName(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),ejAt(ejAt(T,0),1)),ejGet(O,"field"))) requires K ==String "ProjectField" orBool K ==String "AccessField"
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,_,_,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exCheck(ejAt(ejAt(T,0),0) ==K ejString("Collection") andBool ejAt(T,1) ==K exType("UInt64"),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),ejAt(ejAt(T,0),1)) requires K ==String "ProjectIndex" orBool K ==String "AccessIndex"
  rule exPrimitiveType("ConstructEnum",O,S,_,_,_) => exTypeIf(exFirst(exCheck(exHasName(ejGet(S,"enumTypes"),ejGet(O,"enumType")),"TYPE_NAME"),exCheck(exIn(ejGet(O,"member"),exGetName(ejGet(S,"enumTypes"),ejGet(O,"enumType"))),"TYPE_ENUM_MEMBER")),exType("Enum",ejGet(O,"enumType")))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ConstructNone",O,_,_,_,_) => exTyped(exType("Option",ejGet(O,"elementType")))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ConstructSome",O,_,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exCheck(ejAt(T,0) ==K ejGet(O,"elementType"),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exType("Option",ejGet(O,"elementType")))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ConstructCollection",O,_,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exAllTypes(exUnwrapArray(T),ejGet(O,"elementType")),exType("Collection",ejGet(O,"elementType"),ejGet(O,"capacity")))
  rule exPrimitiveType("ConstructRecord",O,S,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exRecordTypeFields(exGetName(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),ejGet(O,"recordType")),exUnwrapArray(ejGet(O,"fields")),exUnwrapArray(T)),exType("Record",ejGet(O,"recordType")))
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,_,_,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exAllTypes(exUnwrapArray(T),exType("Bool")),exType("Bool")) requires K ==String "Not" orBool K ==String "And" orBool K ==String "Or"
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,_,_,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exCheck(ejAt(T,0) ==K ejAt(T,1) andBool (K ==String "Eq" orBool exNumeric(ejAt(T,0))),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exType("Bool")) requires K ==String "Eq" orBool K ==String "Lt" orBool K ==String "Lte" orBool K ==String "Gt" orBool K ==String "Gte"
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,_,_,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exCheck(ejAt(T,0) ==K exType("Bool"),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exType("Unit")) requires K ==String "Require" orBool K ==String "Ensure"
  rule exPrimitiveType("Let",_,_,_,_,_) => exTyped(exType("Unit"))
  rule exPrimitiveType("NextWrite",O,S,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exCheck(ejAt(T,0) ==K ejGet(exGetName(ejGet(S,"fields"),ejGet(O,"field")),"type"),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exType("Unit"))
  rule exPrimitiveType("Emit",O,S,_,_,T) => exTypeIf(exCheck(ejAt(T,0) ==K exType("Record",exGetName(ejGet(S,"operations"),ejGet(O,"operation"))),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exType("Unit"))
  syntax String ::= exAllTypes(EJList,EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exRecordTypeFields(EJSON,EJList,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exAllTypes(ejNil(),_) => ""
  rule exAllTypes(ejCons(T,L),Expected) => exFirst(exCheck(T ==K Expected,"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exAllTypes(L,Expected))
  rule exRecordTypeFields(_,ejNil(),ejNil()) => ""
  rule exRecordTypeFields(S,ejCons(F,L),ejCons(T,R)) => exFirst(exCheck(T ==K exGetName(S,ejGet(F,"name")),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),exRecordTypeFields(S,L,R))
  rule exRecordTypeFields(_,_,_) => "TYPE_MISMATCH" [owise]
  syntax Bool ::= exLiteral(String) [function, total]
  rule exLiteral(K) => K ==String "LitUInt" orBool K ==String "LitSInt" orBool K ==String "LitBool" orBool K ==String "LitText" orBool K ==String "LitAmount" orBool K ==String "LitQuantity" orBool K ==String "LitShares" orBool K ==String "LitRate" orBool K ==String "LitPrice"
  syntax EJSON ::= exLiteralType(String,EJSON) [function, total]
                 | exLiteralValue(String,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exLiteralType("LitUInt",O) => exType("UInt" +String exText(ejGet(O,"width")))
  rule exLiteralType("LitSInt",_) => exType("SInt128")
  rule exLiteralType("LitBool",_) => exType("Bool")
  rule exLiteralType("LitText",_) => exType("Text")
  rule exLiteralType("LitAmount",O) => exType("Amount",ejGet(O,"asset"))
  rule exLiteralType("LitQuantity",O) => exType("Quantity",ejGet(O,"units"),ejGet(O,"scale"))
  rule exLiteralType("LitShares",O) => exType("Shares",ejGet(O,"vault"),ejGet(O,"holder"))
  rule exLiteralType("LitRate",O) => exType("Rate",ejGet(O,"scale"))
  rule exLiteralType("LitPrice",O) => exType("Price",ejGet(O,"base"),ejGet(O,"quote"),ejGet(O,"scale"))
  rule exLiteralType(_,_) => ejMissing() [owise]
  rule exLiteralValue(K,O) => ejGet(O,"mantissa") requires K ==String "LitQuantity" orBool K ==String "LitRate" orBool K ==String "LitPrice"
  rule exLiteralValue(_,O) => ejGet(O,"value") [owise]
  syntax String ::= exText(EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exText(ejString(S)) => S
  rule exText(_) => "" [owise]
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,O,S,_,_,_) => exTypeIf(exFirst(exCheck(K =/=String "LitUInt" orBool ejGet(O,"width") ==K ejString("64") orBool ejGet(O,"width") ==K ejString("128"),"TYPE_LITERAL"),exFirst(exResolve(S,exLiteralType(K,O),"TYPE_LITERAL"),exFirst(exValueDomain(S,exLiteralType(K,O),exLiteralValue(K,O),"TYPE_LITERAL"),exValueBound(S,exLiteralType(K,O),exLiteralValue(K,O),"TYPE_LITERAL")))),exLiteralType(K,O)) requires exLiteral(K)
  rule exPrimitiveType(K,_,S,_,_,T) => exArithmeticType(K,ejAt(T,0),ejAt(T,1),S) requires K ==String "Add" orBool K ==String "Sub" orBool K ==String "Mul" orBool K ==String "FloorDiv" orBool K ==String "CeilDiv"
  syntax EXInference ::= exArithmeticType(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exArithmeticType(K,L,R,S) => exQuantityType(K,L,R,S) requires (K ==String "Mul" orBool K ==String "FloorDiv" orBool K ==String "CeilDiv") andBool ejAt(L,0) ==K ejString("Quantity") andBool ejAt(R,0) ==K ejString("Quantity")
  rule exArithmeticType(K,L,R,_) => exTypeIf(exCheck(L ==K R andBool (L ==K exType("UInt64") orBool L ==K exType("UInt128") orBool L ==K exType("SInt128") orBool ((K ==String "Add" orBool K ==String "Sub") andBool (ejAt(L,0) ==K ejString("Amount") orBool ejAt(L,0) ==K ejString("Shares") orBool ejAt(L,0) ==K ejString("Rate") orBool ejAt(L,0) ==K ejString("Quantity")))),"TYPE_MISMATCH"),L) [owise]
  syntax EXInference ::= exQuantityType(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
                       | exQuantityResult(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  syntax Int ::= exDirection(String) [function, total]
  rule exDirection("Mul") => 1
  rule exDirection(_) => -1 [owise]
  rule exQuantityType(K,L,R,S) => exQuantityResult(S,exType("Quantity",ejArray(exMergeUnits(exUnwrapArray(ejAt(L,1)),exUnwrapArray(ejAt(R,1)),exDirection(K))),ejString(Int2String(exInt(ejAt(L,2)) +Int exDirection(K) *Int exInt(ejAt(R,2))))))
  rule exQuantityResult(S,T) => exTypeIf(exResolve(S,T,"TYPE_QUANTITY_DOMAIN"),T)
  syntax EJList ::= exMergeUnits(EJList,EJList,Int) [function, total]
                  | exSignedUnits(EJList,Int) [function, total]
                  | exUnitCons(String,Int,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exUnitCons(_,0,L) => L
  rule exUnitCons(N,P,L) => ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(N),ejCons(ejString(Int2String(P)),ejNil()))),L) requires P =/=Int 0
  rule exSignedUnits(ejNil(),_) => ejNil()
  rule exSignedUnits(ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(N),ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L),D) => exUnitCons(N,D *Int exInt(P),exSignedUnits(L,D))
  rule exMergeUnits(L,ejNil(),_) => L
  rule exMergeUnits(ejNil(),R,D) => exSignedUnits(R,D) requires R =/=K ejNil()
  rule exMergeUnits(ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(A),ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L),ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(B),ejCons(Q,ejNil()))),R),D) => exUnitCons(A,exInt(P),exMergeUnits(L,ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(B),ejCons(Q,ejNil()))),R),D)) requires A <String B
  rule exMergeUnits(ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(A),ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L),ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(B),ejCons(Q,ejNil()))),R),D) => exUnitCons(B,D *Int exInt(Q),exMergeUnits(ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(A),ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L),R,D)) requires A >String B
  rule exMergeUnits(ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(A),ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L),ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(A),ejCons(Q,ejNil()))),R),D) => exUnitCons(A,exInt(P) +Int D *Int exInt(Q),exMergeUnits(L,R,D))
  rule exMergeUnits(_,_,_) => ejNil() [owise]
  rule exPrimitiveType(_,_,_,_,_,_) => exTypeError("TYPE_CONSTRUCTOR",exSynthetic(),ejNil()) [owise]
endmodule

=== experiments/moriarty-language/formal/k/expression-json.k sha256 c5fdff903f8a1a77bba3dd3efd0cf2237946fc2fdd5c9ac6a71064fa0124603d ===
// Structural JSON terms. No checked/type/evaluation metadata is accepted from the codec.
module MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-JSON
  imports INT
  imports BOOL
  imports STRING
  syntax EJSON ::= ejNull() [symbol(ejNull)]
                 | ejBool(Bool) [symbol(ejBool)]
                 | ejNumber(Int) [symbol(ejNumber)]
                 | ejString(String) [symbol(ejString)]
                 | ejArray(EJList) [symbol(ejArray)]
                 | ejObject(EJList) [symbol(ejObject)]
                 | ejPair(String, EJSON) [symbol(ejPair)]
                 | ejMissing() [symbol(ejMissing)]
  syntax EJList ::= ejNil() [symbol(ejNil)]
                  | ejCons(EJSON, EJList) [symbol(ejCons)]
  syntax ERequest ::= expressionRequest(EJSON, EJSON) [symbol(expressionRequest)]
  syntax EJSON ::= ejGet(EJSON, String) [function, total]
                 | ejFind(EJList, String) [function, total]
                 | ejAt(EJSON, Int) [function, total]
                 | ejNth(EJList, Int) [function, total]
  rule ejGet(ejObject(L), K) => ejFind(L, K)
  rule ejGet(_, _) => ejMissing() [owise]
  rule ejFind(ejNil(), _) => ejMissing()
  rule ejFind(ejCons(ejPair(K,V),_), K) => V
  rule ejFind(ejCons(_,L), K) => ejFind(L,K) [owise]
  rule ejAt(ejArray(L), I) => ejNth(L,I)
  rule ejAt(_,_) => ejMissing() [owise]
  rule ejNth(ejCons(V,_),0) => V
  rule ejNth(ejCons(_,L),I) => ejNth(L,I -Int 1) requires I >Int 0
  rule ejNth(_,_) => ejMissing() [owise]
  syntax Int ::= ejLength(EJList) [function, total]
               | ejCount(EJSON) [function, total]
               | ejCountList(EJList) [function, total]
               | ejDepth(EJSON) [function, total]
               | ejDepthList(EJList) [function, total]
  rule ejLength(ejNil()) => 0
  rule ejLength(ejCons(_,L)) => 1 +Int ejLength(L)
  // Object keys are not extra JSON-tree value nodes.
  rule ejCount(ejArray(L)) => 1 +Int ejCountList(L)
  rule ejCount(ejObject(L)) => 1 +Int ejCountList(L)
  rule ejCount(ejPair(_,V)) => ejCount(V)
  rule ejCount(_) => 1 [owise]
  rule ejCountList(ejNil()) => 0
  rule ejCountList(ejCons(V,L)) => ejCount(V) +Int ejCountList(L)
  rule ejDepth(ejArray(L)) => 1 +Int ejDepthList(L)
  rule ejDepth(ejObject(L)) => 1 +Int ejDepthList(L)
  rule ejDepth(ejPair(_,V)) => ejDepth(V)
  rule ejDepth(_) => 1 [owise]
  rule ejDepthList(ejNil()) => 0
  rule ejDepthList(ejCons(V,L)) => maxInt(ejDepth(V),ejDepthList(L))
  syntax Bool ::= ejHas(EJSON,String) [function, total]
                | ejMember(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                | ejClosed(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                | ejKeysMatch(EJList,EJList) [function, total]
                | ejIsString(EJSON) [function, total]
                | ejIsObject(EJSON) [function, total]
                | ejIsArray(EJSON) [function, total]
  rule ejHas(V,K) => ejGet(V,K) =/=K ejMissing()
  rule ejMember(V,ejCons(V,_)) => true
  rule ejMember(V,ejCons(_,L)) => ejMember(V,L) [owise]
  rule ejMember(_,ejNil()) => false
  rule ejClosed(ejObject(L),Keys) => ejLength(L) ==Int ejLength(Keys) andBool ejKeysMatch(L,Keys)
  rule ejClosed(_,_) => false [owise]
  rule ejKeysMatch(ejNil(),_) => true
  rule ejKeysMatch(ejCons(ejPair(K,_),L),Keys) => ejMember(ejString(K),Keys) andBool ejKeysMatch(L,Keys)
  rule ejKeysMatch(_,_) => false [owise]
  rule ejIsString(ejString(_)) => true
  rule ejIsString(_) => false [owise]
  rule ejIsObject(ejObject(_)) => true
  rule ejIsObject(_) => false [owise]
  rule ejIsArray(ejArray(_)) => true
  rule ejIsArray(_) => false [owise]
  syntax EJList ::= ejAppend(EJList,EJList) [function, total]
  rule ejAppend(ejNil(),R) => R
  rule ejAppend(ejCons(V,L),R) => ejCons(V,ejAppend(L,R))
  syntax EJSON ::= ejPut(EJSON,String,EJSON) [function, total]
  syntax EJList ::= ejPutPair(EJList,String,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule ejPut(ejObject(L),K,V) => ejObject(ejPutPair(L,K,V))
  rule ejPut(_,_,_) => ejMissing() [owise]
  rule ejPutPair(ejNil(),K,V) => ejCons(ejPair(K,V),ejNil())
  rule ejPutPair(ejCons(ejPair(K,_),L),K,V) => ejCons(ejPair(K,V),L)
  rule ejPutPair(ejCons(ejPair(A,X),L),K,V) => ejCons(ejPair(K,V),ejCons(ejPair(A,X),L)) requires K <String A
  rule ejPutPair(ejCons(ejPair(A,X),L),K,V) => ejCons(ejPair(A,X),ejPutPair(L,K,V)) requires K >String A
  rule ejPutPair(ejCons(X,L),K,V) => ejCons(X,ejPutPair(L,K,V)) [owise]
endmodule

=== experiments/moriarty-language/formal/k/expression-schema.k sha256 90d20d8d68553143d8b176eded6f495d0332c02cd54561444bdb49ae2edbd7e7 ===
requires "expression-types.k"
module MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-SCHEMA
  imports MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-TYPES
  syntax EJList ::= exSchemaKeys() [function]
  rule exSchemaKeys() => ejCons(ejString("units"),ejCons(ejString("assets"),ejCons(ejString("vaults"),ejCons(ejString("parties"),ejCons(ejString("recordTypes"),ejCons(ejString("enumTypes"),ejCons(ejString("fields"),ejCons(ejString("args"),ejCons(ejString("observations"),ejCons(ejString("operations"),ejNil()))))))))))
  syntax String ::= exSchemaShape(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exSchemaMaps(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exRosterShape(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exNames(EJList) [function, total]
                  | exMapShape(EJSON,String) [function, total]
                  | exMapItems(EJList,String) [function, total]
                  | exMapItem(EJSON,String) [function, total]
  rule exSchemaShape(S) => exFirst(exCheck(ejClosed(S,exSchemaKeys()),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exFirst(exCheck(exJsonBytes(S) <=Int 65536 andBool ejCount(S) <=Int 4096 andBool ejDepth(S) <=Int 64,"INPUT_BOUND"),exSchemaMapNames(S)))
  rule exSchemaMaps(S) => exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"units")),exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"assets")),exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"vaults")),exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"parties")),exFirst(exMapShape(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),"record"),exFirst(exMapShape(ejGet(S,"enumTypes"),"enum"),exFirst(exMapShape(ejGet(S,"fields"),"field"),exFirst(exMapShape(ejGet(S,"args"),"type"),exFirst(exMapShape(ejGet(S,"observations"),"type"),exMapShape(ejGet(S,"operations"),"name"))))))))))
  rule exRosterShape(ejArray(L)) => exNames(L)
  rule exRosterShape(_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exNames(ejNil()) => ""
  rule exNames(ejCons(V,L)) => exFirst(exCheck(exIdentifier(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exNames(L))
  rule exMapShape(ejObject(L),K) => exMapItems(L,K)
  rule exMapShape(_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exMapItems(ejNil(),_) => ""
  rule exMapItems(ejCons(ejPair(N,V),L),K) => exFirst(exCheck(exIdentifier(ejString(N)),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exFirst(exMapItem(V,K),exMapItems(L,K)))
  rule exMapItems(_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exMapItem(ejObject(L),"record") => exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejObject(L)),exFirst(exCheck(ejLength(L) <=Int 64,"INPUT_BOUND"),exMapItems(L,"type")))
  rule exMapItem(ejArray(L),"enum") => exFirst(exCheck(ejLength(L) <=Int 128,"INPUT_BOUND"),exNames(L))
  rule exMapItem(V,"field") => exFirst(exCheck(ejClosed(V,ejCons(ejString("type"),ejCons(ejString("writeClass"),ejNil()))),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exFirst(exCheck(exTypeShape(ejGet(V,"type")),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exCheck(ejGet(V,"writeClass") ==K ejString("ordinary") orBool ejGet(V,"writeClass") ==K ejString("financial"),"INPUT_SCHEMA")))
  rule exMapItem(V,"type") => exCheck(exTypeShape(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMapItem(V,"name") => exCheck(exIdentifier(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMapItem(_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  syntax Int ::= exDeclarations(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
               | exSize(EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exSize(ejObject(L)) => ejLength(L)
  rule exSize(ejArray(L)) => ejLength(L)
  rule exSize(_) => 0 [owise]
  rule exDeclarations(_,ejNil()) => 0
  rule exDeclarations(S,ejCons(ejString(K),L)) => exSize(ejGet(S,K)) +Int exDeclarations(S,L)
  syntax String ::= exSorted(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exSortedList(EJList,String) [function, total]
                  | exSortedEnums(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exSortedEnumList(EJList) [function, total]
                  | exSchemaDomain(EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exSorted(ejArray(L)) => exSortedList(L,"")
  rule exSorted(_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exSortedList(ejNil(),_) => ""
  rule exSortedList(ejCons(ejString(N),L),P) => exFirst(exCheck(N >String P,"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSortedList(L,N))
  rule exSortedList(_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exSortedEnums(ejObject(L)) => exSortedEnumList(L)
  rule exSortedEnums(_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exSortedEnumList(ejNil()) => ""
  rule exSortedEnumList(ejCons(ejPair(_,V),L)) => exFirst(exSorted(V),exSortedEnumList(L))
  rule exSortedEnumList(_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exSchemaDomain(S) => exFirst(exCheck(exDeclarations(S,exSchemaKeys()) <=Int 256,"INPUT_BOUND"),exFirst(exSorted(ejGet(S,"units")),exFirst(exSorted(ejGet(S,"assets")),exFirst(exSorted(ejGet(S,"vaults")),exFirst(exSorted(ejGet(S,"parties")),exFirst(exSortedEnums(ejGet(S,"enumTypes")),exFirst(exDefinitions(S,exDefinitionList(S)),exSchemaCycles(S))))))))
  // Definition resolution order is the lexical name order from contract S0;
  // record field keys are qualified as Record.field before sorting.
  syntax EJList ::= exDefinitionList(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exDefinitionsFrom(EJSON,String,String) [function, total]
                  | exDefinitionsItems(EJList,String,String) [function, total]
                  | exSortPairs(EJList) [function, total]
                  | exInsertPair(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exDefinitionList(S) => exSortPairs(ejAppend(exDefinitionsFrom(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),"records",""),ejAppend(exDefinitionsFrom(ejGet(S,"operations"),"operations",""),ejAppend(exDefinitionsFrom(ejGet(S,"args"),"types",""),ejAppend(exDefinitionsFrom(ejGet(S,"observations"),"types",""),exDefinitionsFrom(ejGet(S,"fields"),"fields",""))))))
  rule exDefinitionsFrom(ejObject(L),K,P) => exDefinitionsItems(L,K,P)
  rule exDefinitionsFrom(_,_,_) => ejNil() [owise]
  rule exDefinitionsItems(ejNil(),_,_) => ejNil()
  rule exDefinitionsItems(ejCons(ejPair(N,V),L),"records",_) => ejAppend(exDefinitionsFrom(V,"types",N +String "."),exDefinitionsItems(L,"records",""))
  rule exDefinitionsItems(ejCons(ejPair(N,V),L),"types",P) => ejCons(ejPair(P +String N,V),exDefinitionsItems(L,"types",P))
  rule exDefinitionsItems(ejCons(ejPair(N,V),L),"operations",_) => ejCons(ejPair(N,exType("Record",V)),exDefinitionsItems(L,"operations",""))
  rule exDefinitionsItems(ejCons(ejPair(N,V),L),"fields",_) => ejCons(ejPair(N,ejGet(V,"type")),exDefinitionsItems(L,"fields",""))
  rule exDefinitionsItems(_,_,_) => ejNil() [owise]
  rule exSortPairs(ejNil()) => ejNil()
  rule exSortPairs(ejCons(V,L)) => exInsertPair(V,exSortPairs(L))
  rule exInsertPair(V,ejNil()) => ejCons(V,ejNil())
  rule exInsertPair(ejPair(A,V),ejCons(ejPair(B,W),L)) => ejCons(ejPair(A,V),ejCons(ejPair(B,W),L)) requires A <=String B
  rule exInsertPair(ejPair(A,V),ejCons(ejPair(B,W),L)) => ejCons(ejPair(B,W),exInsertPair(ejPair(A,V),L)) requires A >String B
  rule exInsertPair(_,L) => L [owise]
  syntax String ::= exDefinitions(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exSchemaCycles(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exCycleDefinitions(EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exCycleType(EJSON,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exCycleNamed(EJSON,String,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exCycleFields(EJSON,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exCycleFieldList(EJSON,EJList,EJList) [function, total]
  rule exDefinitions(_,ejNil()) => ""
  rule exDefinitions(S,ejCons(ejPair(_,T),L)) => exFirst(exResolve(S,T,"TYPE_LITERAL"),exDefinitions(S,L))
  // Every type is resolved first. Finite DFS with a path set detects Record and
  // Operation cycles through arbitrary Option/Collection wrappers.
  rule exSchemaCycles(S) => exCycleDefinitions(S,exDefinitionList(S))
  rule exCycleDefinitions(_,ejNil()) => ""
  rule exCycleDefinitions(S,ejCons(ejPair(_,T),L)) => exFirst(exCycleType(S,T,ejNil()),exCycleDefinitions(S,L))
  rule exCycleType(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Option"),ejCons(T,ejNil()))),P) => exCycleType(S,T,P)
  rule exCycleType(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Collection"),ejCons(T,ejCons(_,ejNil())))),P) => exCycleType(S,T,P)
  rule exCycleType(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Record"),ejCons(ejString(N),ejNil()))),P) => exCycleNamed(S,"R:" +String N,P)
  rule exCycleType(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Operation"),ejCons(ejString(N),ejNil()))),P) => exCycleNamed(S,"O:" +String N,P)
  rule exCycleType(_,_,_) => "" [owise]
  rule exCycleNamed(_,N,P) => "TYPE_SCHEMA_CYCLE" requires ejMember(ejString(N),P)
  rule exCycleNamed(S,N,P) => exCycleFields(S,ejGet(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),exSubstring(N,2,lengthString(N))),ejCons(ejString(N),P)) requires notBool ejMember(ejString(N),P) andBool exSubstring(N,0,2) ==String "R:"
  rule exCycleNamed(S,N,P) => exCycleType(S,exType("Record",ejGet(ejGet(S,"operations"),exSubstring(N,2,lengthString(N)))),ejCons(ejString(N),P)) requires notBool ejMember(ejString(N),P) andBool exSubstring(N,0,2) ==String "O:"
  rule exCycleNamed(_,_,_) => "TYPE_NAME" [owise]
  rule exCycleFields(S,ejObject(L),P) => exCycleFieldList(S,L,P)
  rule exCycleFields(_,_,_) => "TYPE_NAME" [owise]
  rule exCycleFieldList(_,ejNil(),_) => ""
  rule exCycleFieldList(S,ejCons(ejPair(_,T),L),P) => exFirst(exCycleType(S,T,P),exCycleFieldList(S,L,P))
  syntax String ::= exSchemaMapNames(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exMapNamesOnly(EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exPairNamesOnly(EJList) [function, total]
  rule exSchemaMapNames(S) => exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"units")),exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"assets")),exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"vaults")),exFirst(exRosterShape(ejGet(S,"parties")),exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejGet(S,"recordTypes")),exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejGet(S,"enumTypes")),exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejGet(S,"fields")),exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejGet(S,"args")),exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejGet(S,"observations")),exFirst(exMapNamesOnly(ejGet(S,"operations")),exSchemaMaps(S)))))))))))
  rule exMapNamesOnly(ejObject(L)) => exPairNamesOnly(L)
  rule exMapNamesOnly(_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exPairNamesOnly(ejNil()) => ""
  rule exPairNamesOnly(ejCons(ejPair(N,_),L)) => exFirst(exCheck(exIdentifier(ejString(N)),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exPairNamesOnly(L))
  rule exPairNamesOnly(_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
endmodule

requires "expression-types.k"
module MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-SHAPE
  imports MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-TYPES
  syntax EXRole ::= exRole(String,String)
  syntax EXRoles ::= exRolesNil() | exRolesCons(EXRole,EXRoles)
  syntax EXRoles ::= exRoles(String) [function, total]
  rule exRoles("LitUInt") => exRolesCons(exRole("width","int"),exRolesCons(exRole("value","int"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("LitSInt") => exRolesCons(exRole("value","int"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("LitBool") => exRolesCons(exRole("value","bool"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("LitText") => exRolesCons(exRole("value","text"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("LitAmount") => exRolesCons(exRole("asset","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("value","int"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("LitQuantity") => exRolesCons(exRole("units","units"),exRolesCons(exRole("scale","int"),exRolesCons(exRole("mantissa","int"),exRolesNil())))
  rule exRoles("LitShares") => exRolesCons(exRole("vault","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("holder","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("value","int"),exRolesNil())))
  rule exRoles("LitRate") => exRolesCons(exRole("scale","int"),exRolesCons(exRole("mantissa","int"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("LitPrice") => exRolesCons(exRole("base","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("quote","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("scale","int"),exRolesCons(exRole("mantissa","int"),exRolesNil()))))
  rule exRoles("ReadLocal") => exRolesCons(exRole("name","id"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("ReadArg") => exRolesCons(exRole("name","id"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("ReadObs") => exRolesCons(exRole("name","id"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("ReadPre") => exRolesCons(exRole("view","view"),exRolesCons(exRole("field","id"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("ProjectField") => exRolesCons(exRole("record","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("field","id"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("AccessField") => exRolesCons(exRole("record","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("field","id"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("ProjectIndex") => exRolesCons(exRole("collection","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("index","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("AccessIndex") => exRolesCons(exRole("collection","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("index","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("ConstructRecord") => exRolesCons(exRole("recordType","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("fields","fields"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("ConstructEnum") => exRolesCons(exRole("enumType","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("member","id"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("ConstructSome") => exRolesCons(exRole("elementType","type"),exRolesCons(exRole("value","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("ConstructNone") => exRolesCons(exRole("elementType","type"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("ConstructCollection") => exRolesCons(exRole("elementType","type"),exRolesCons(exRole("capacity","int"),exRolesCons(exRole("items","list"),exRolesNil())))
  rule exRoles("Add") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Sub") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Mul") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("FloorDiv") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("CeilDiv") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Eq") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Lt") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Lte") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Gt") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Gte") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Not") => exRolesCons(exRole("value","expr"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("And") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Or") => exRolesCons(exRole("left","expr"),exRolesCons(exRole("right","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Require") => exRolesCons(exRole("condition","expr"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("Ensure") => exRolesCons(exRole("condition","expr"),exRolesNil())
  rule exRoles("Let") => exRolesCons(exRole("name","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("value","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("NextWrite") => exRolesCons(exRole("field","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("value","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles("Emit") => exRolesCons(exRole("operation","id"),exRolesCons(exRole("fields","expr"),exRolesNil()))
  rule exRoles(_) => exRolesNil() [owise]
  syntax Bool ::= exConstructor(String) [function, total]
  rule exConstructor("LitUInt") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitSInt") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitBool") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitText") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitAmount") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitQuantity") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitShares") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitRate") => true
  rule exConstructor("LitPrice") => true
  rule exConstructor("ReadLocal") => true
  rule exConstructor("ReadArg") => true
  rule exConstructor("ReadObs") => true
  rule exConstructor("ReadPre") => true
  rule exConstructor("ProjectField") => true
  rule exConstructor("AccessField") => true
  rule exConstructor("ProjectIndex") => true
  rule exConstructor("AccessIndex") => true
  rule exConstructor("ConstructRecord") => true
  rule exConstructor("ConstructEnum") => true
  rule exConstructor("ConstructSome") => true
  rule exConstructor("ConstructNone") => true
  rule exConstructor("ConstructCollection") => true
  rule exConstructor("Add") => true
  rule exConstructor("Sub") => true
  rule exConstructor("Mul") => true
  rule exConstructor("FloorDiv") => true
  rule exConstructor("CeilDiv") => true
  rule exConstructor("Eq") => true

  rule exConstructor("Gte") => true

  syntax EJList ::= exRoleNames(EXRoles) [function, total]
  rule exRoleNames(exRolesNil()) => ejNil()
  rule exRoleNames(exRolesCons(exRole(N,_),R)) => ejCons(ejString(N),exRoleNames(R))
  syntax EXSite ::= exGood() | exBad(String,EJSON,EJList)
  syntax EXSite ::= exThen(EXSite,EXSite) [function, total]
                  | exAtCode(String,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exLocate(EXSite,EJSON,EJList) [function, total]
                  | exCoreShape(EJSON,Int) [function, total]
                  | exShapeNode(EJSON,EJList,Int,Int,Int) [function, total]
                  | exShapeFields(EJSON,EJList,Int,Int,Int) [function, total]
                  | exShapeNamed(EJSON,EJSON,EJList,Int,Int,Int) [function, total]
                  | exShapeOperands(EXRoles,EJSON,EJList,Int,Int,Int,Int) [function, total]
                  | exShapeList(EJList,EJList,Int,Int,Int,Int,Bool) [function, total]
                  | exShapeAction(EJList,Int,Int,Int) [function, total]
  rule exThen(exGood(),R) => R
  rule exThen(exBad(C,S,P),_) => exBad(C,S,P)
  rule exAtCode("",_,_) => exGood()
  rule exAtCode(C,S,P) => exBad(C,S,P) requires C =/=String ""
  rule exLocate(exBad(C,_,P),S,P) => exBad(C,S,P)
  rule exLocate(R,_,_) => R [owise]
  rule exCoreShape(N,B) => exActionShape(N,B) requires ejHas(N,"statements")
  rule exCoreShape(N,B) => exShapeNode(N,ejNil(),1,B,0) requires notBool ejHas(N,"statements")
  syntax EXSite ::= exActionShape(EJSON,Int) [function, total]
  rule exActionShape(N,B) => exThen(exAtCode(exFirst(exCheck(ejClosed(N,ejCons(ejString("statements"),ejCons(ejString("span"),ejNil()))),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exFirst(exSpanCode(ejGet(N,"span"),B),exFirst(exCheck(ejIsArray(ejGet(N,"statements")),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exCheck(exArrayLength(ejGet(N,"statements")) <=Int 256,"INPUT_BOUND")))),exSafeSpan(ejGet(N,"span"),B),ejNil()),exShapeAction(exArrayItems(ejGet(N,"statements")),B,0,0))
  rule exShapeAction(ejNil(),_,_,_) => exGood()
  rule exShapeAction(ejCons(N,L),B,I,Seen) => exThen(exShapeNode(N,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil()),1,B,Seen),exShapeAction(L,B,I +Int 1,Seen +Int exASTSize(N)))
  rule exShapeNode(N,P,D,B,Seen) => exBad("INPUT_BOUND",exSafeSpan(ejGet(N,"span"),B),P) requires D >Int 64 orBool Seen >=Int 4096
  rule exShapeNode(N,P,D,B,Seen) => exThen(exAtCode(exCheck(ejClosed(N,ejCons(ejString("constructor"),ejCons(ejString("operands"),ejCons(ejString("span"),ejNil())))),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSafeSpan(ejGet(N,"span"),B),P),exShapeFields(N,P,D,B,Seen)) requires D <=Int 64 andBool Seen <Int 4096
  rule exShapeFields(N,P,D,B,Seen) => exThen(exAtCode(exSpanCode(ejGet(N,"span"),B),exSafeSpan(ejGet(N,"span"),B),P),exShapeNamed(ejGet(N,"constructor"),N,P,D,B,Seen))
  rule exShapeNamed(ejString(K),N,P,D,B,Seen) => exLocate(exThen(exAtCode(exCheck(exConstructor(K),"TYPE_CONSTRUCTOR"),exSynthetic(),P),exThen(exAtCode(exCheck(ejClosed(ejGet(N,"operands"),exRoleNames(exRoles(K))),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),P),exShapeOperands(exRoles(K),ejGet(N,"operands"),P,D,B,0,Seen +Int 1))),exSafeSpan(ejGet(N,"span"),B),P)
  rule exShapeNamed(_,N,P,_,B,_) => exBad("TYPE_CONSTRUCTOR",exSafeSpan(ejGet(N,"span"),B),P) [owise]
  rule exShapeOperands(exRolesNil(),_,_,_,_,_,_) => exGood()
  rule exShapeOperands(exRolesCons(exRole(N,"expr"),R),O,P,D,B,I,Seen) => exThen(exShapeNode(ejGet(O,N),ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())),D +Int 1,B,Seen),exShapeOperands(R,O,P,D,B,I +Int 1,Seen +Int exASTSize(ejGet(O,N))))
  rule exShapeOperands(exRolesCons(exRole(N,K),R),O,P,D,B,I,Seen) => exThen(exAtCode(exMetaShape(K,ejGet(O,N)),exSynthetic(),P),exShapeOperands(R,O,P,D,B,I,Seen)) requires K =/=String "expr" andBool K =/=String "list" andBool K =/=String "fields"
  rule exShapeOperands(exRolesCons(exRole(N,K),R),O,P,D,B,I,Seen) => exThen(exAtCode(exFirst(exCheck(ejIsArray(ejGet(O,N)),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exCheck(exArrayLength(ejGet(O,N)) <=Int exListLimit(K),"INPUT_BOUND")),exSynthetic(),P),exThen(exShapeList(exArrayItems(ejGet(O,N)),P,D,B,I,Seen,K ==String "fields"),exShapeOperands(R,O,P,D,B,I +Int exArrayLength(ejGet(O,N)),Seen +Int exASTListSize(exArrayItems(ejGet(O,N)),K ==String "fields")))) requires K ==String "list" orBool K ==String "fields"
  rule exShapeList(ejNil(),_,_,_,_,_,_) => exGood()
  rule exShapeList(ejCons(N,L),P,D,B,I,Seen,false) => exThen(exShapeNode(N,ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())),D +Int 1,B,Seen),exShapeList(L,P,D,B,I +Int 1,Seen +Int exASTSize(N),false))
  rule exShapeList(ejCons(N,L),P,D,B,I,Seen,true) => exThen(exAtCode(exCheck(ejClosed(N,ejCons(ejString("name"),ejCons(ejString("value"),ejNil()))) andBool exIdentifier(ejGet(N,"name")),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),P),exThen(exShapeNode(ejGet(N,"value"),ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())),D +Int 1,B,Seen),exShapeList(L,P,D,B,I +Int 1,Seen +Int exASTSize(ejGet(N,"value")),true)))
  syntax Int ::= exArrayLength(EJSON) [function, total]
               | exListLimit(String) [function, total]
               | exASTSize(EJSON) [function, total]
               | exASTRoles(EXRoles,EJSON) [function, total]
               | exASTListSize(EJList,Bool) [function, total]
  syntax EJList ::= exArrayItems(EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exArrayLength(ejArray(L)) => ejLength(L)
  rule exArrayLength(_) => 0 [owise]
  rule exArrayItems(ejArray(L)) => L
  rule exArrayItems(_) => ejNil() [owise]
  rule exListLimit("fields") => 64
  rule exListLimit(_) => 128 [owise]
  rule exASTSize(N) => 1 +Int exASTName(ejGet(N,"constructor"),ejGet(N,"operands"))
  syntax Int ::= exASTName(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exASTName(ejString(K),O) => exASTRoles(exRoles(K),O)
  rule exASTName(_,_) => 0 [owise]
  rule exASTRoles(exRolesNil(),_) => 0
  rule exASTRoles(exRolesCons(exRole(N,"expr"),R),O) => exASTSize(ejGet(O,N)) +Int exASTRoles(R,O)
  rule exASTRoles(exRolesCons(exRole(N,K),R),O) => exASTListSize(exArrayItems(ejGet(O,N)),K ==String "fields") +Int exASTRoles(R,O) requires K ==String "list" orBool K ==String "fields"
  rule exASTRoles(exRolesCons(exRole(_,K),R),O) => exASTRoles(R,O) requires K =/=String "expr" andBool K =/=String "list" andBool K =/=String "fields"
  rule exASTListSize(ejNil(),_) => 0
  rule exASTListSize(ejCons(N,L),false) => exASTSize(N) +Int exASTListSize(L,false)
  rule exASTListSize(ejCons(N,L),true) => exASTSize(ejGet(N,"value")) +Int exASTListSize(L,true)
  syntax String ::= exMetaShape(String,EJSON) [function, total]
                  | exSpanCode(EJSON,Int) [function, total]
  rule exMetaShape("id",V) => exCheck(exIdentifier(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape("int",V) => exCheck(exDecimal(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape("text",V) => exCheck(ejIsString(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape("bool",V) => exCheck(V ==K ejBool(true) orBool V ==K ejBool(false),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape("view",V) => exCheck(V ==K ejString("pre") orBool V ==K ejString("post") orBool V ==K ejString("next"),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape("type",V) => exCheck(exTypeShape(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape("units",V) => exCheck(exUnitShape(V),"INPUT_SCHEMA")
  rule exMetaShape(_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  rule exSpanCode(S,B) => exFirst(exCheck(ejClosed(S,ejCons(ejString("kind"),ejCons(ejString("start"),ejCons(ejString("end"),ejNil())))) andBool exDecimal(ejGet(S,"start")) andBool exDecimal(ejGet(S,"end")) andBool (ejGet(S,"kind") ==K ejString("source") orBool ejGet(S,"kind") ==K ejString("synthetic")),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exCheck(exSpan(S,B),"INPUT_SPAN"))
endmodule

=== experiments/moriarty-language/formal/k/expression-types.k sha256 4605ac6d1a61577fa87afe5960cf94ca3355ea356a149bfb3f1c8efef63ce0f8 ===
requires "expression-wire.k"
module MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-TYPES
  imports MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-WIRE
  syntax EJSON ::= exType(String) [function]
                 | exType(String,EJSON) [function]
                 | exType(String,EJSON,EJSON) [function]
                 | exType(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJSON) [function]
  rule exType(K) => ejArray(ejCons(ejString(K),ejNil()))
  rule exType(K,A) => ejArray(ejCons(ejString(K),ejCons(A,ejNil())))
  rule exType(K,A,B) => ejArray(ejCons(ejString(K),ejCons(A,ejCons(B,ejNil()))))
  rule exType(K,A,B,C) => ejArray(ejCons(ejString(K),ejCons(A,ejCons(B,ejCons(C,ejNil())))))
  syntax Bool ::= exNumeric(EJSON) [function, total]
                | exFits(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
                | exTypeShape(EJSON) [function, total]
                | exUnitShape(EJSON) [function, total]
                | exUnitShapeList(EJList) [function, total]
  rule exNumeric(T) => ejMember(ejAt(T,0),ejCons(ejString("UInt64"),ejCons(ejString("UInt128"),ejCons(ejString("SInt128"),ejCons(ejString("Amount"),ejCons(ejString("Shares"),ejCons(ejString("Rate"),ejCons(ejString("Price"),ejCons(ejString("Quantity"),ejNil())))))))))
  rule exFits(T,V) => exDecimal(V) andBool exInt(V) >=Int (0 -Int (2 ^Int 127)) andBool exInt(V) <Int (2 ^Int 127)
    requires ejAt(T,0) ==K ejString("SInt128") orBool ejAt(T,0) ==K ejString("Rate") orBool ejAt(T,0) ==K ejString("Quantity")
  rule exFits(T,V) => exDecimal(V) andBool exInt(V) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(V) <Int (2 ^Int 64) requires ejAt(T,0) ==K ejString("UInt64")
  rule exFits(T,V) => exDecimal(V) andBool exInt(V) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(V) <Int (2 ^Int 128) [owise]
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(K),ejNil()))) => K ==String "UInt64" orBool K ==String "UInt128" orBool K ==String "SInt128" orBool K ==String "Bool" orBool K ==String "Text" orBool K ==String "Unit"
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(K),ejCons(N,ejNil())))) => exIdentifier(N) requires K ==String "Amount" orBool K ==String "Record" orBool K ==String "Enum" orBool K ==String "Operation"
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Option"),ejCons(T,ejNil())))) => exTypeShape(T)
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Rate"),ejCons(S,ejNil())))) => exDecimal(S)
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Shares"),ejCons(V,ejCons(H,ejNil()))))) => exIdentifier(V) andBool exIdentifier(H)
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Collection"),ejCons(T,ejCons(N,ejNil()))))) => exTypeShape(T) andBool exDecimal(N)
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Quantity"),ejCons(U,ejCons(S,ejNil()))))) => exUnitShape(U) andBool exDecimal(S)
  rule exTypeShape(ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Price"),ejCons(A,ejCons(B,ejCons(S,ejNil())))))) => exIdentifier(A) andBool exIdentifier(B) andBool exDecimal(S)
  rule exTypeShape(_) => false [owise]
  rule exUnitShape(ejArray(L)) => exUnitShapeList(L)
  rule exUnitShape(_) => false [owise]
  rule exUnitShapeList(ejNil()) => true
  rule exUnitShapeList(ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(N,ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L)) => exIdentifier(N) andBool exDecimal(P) andBool exUnitShapeList(L)
  rule exUnitShapeList(_) => false [owise]

  // Empty string is success; all failures remain closed contract codes.
  syntax String ::= exFirst(String,String) [function, total]
                  | exCheck(Bool,String) [function, total]
                  | exResolve(EJSON,EJSON,String) [function, total]
                  | exResolveBase(EJSON,EJSON,String) [function, total]
                  | exUnits(EJSON,EJList,String,String) [function, total]
  rule exFirst("",B) => B
  rule exFirst(A,_) => A requires A =/=String ""
  rule exCheck(true,_) => ""
  rule exCheck(false,C) => C
  rule exResolve(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Option"),ejCons(T,ejNil()))),C) => exResolve(S,T,C)
  rule exResolve(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Collection"),ejCons(T,ejCons(N,ejNil())))),C) => exFirst(exResolve(S,T,C),exCheck(exInt(N) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(N) <=Int 128,"TYPE_COLLECTION_BOUND"))
  rule exResolve(S,T,C) => exResolveBase(S,T,C) [owise]
  rule exResolveBase(_,T,_) => "" requires T ==K exType("UInt64") orBool T ==K exType("UInt128") orBool T ==K exType("SInt128") orBool T ==K exType("Bool") orBool T ==K exType("Text")
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Amount"),ejCons(A,ejNil()))),_) => exCheck(exIn(A,ejGet(S,"assets")),"TYPE_NAME")
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Shares"),ejCons(V,ejCons(H,ejNil())))),_) => exCheck(exIn(V,ejGet(S,"vaults")) andBool exIn(H,ejGet(S,"parties")),"TYPE_NAME")
  rule exResolveBase(_,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Rate"),ejCons(N,ejNil()))),C) => exCheck(exInt(N) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(N) <=Int 18,C)
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Quantity"),ejCons(ejArray(U),ejCons(N,ejNil())))),C) => exFirst(exCheck(ejLength(U) <=Int 8,C),exFirst(exUnits(S,U,"",C),exCheck(exInt(N) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(N) <=Int 18,C)))
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Price"),ejCons(A,ejCons(B,ejCons(N,ejNil()))))),C) => exFirst(exCheck(exIn(A,ejGet(S,"assets")) andBool exIn(B,ejGet(S,"assets")),"TYPE_NAME"),exCheck(A =/=K B andBool exInt(N) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(N) <=Int 18,C))
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Record"),ejCons(ejString(N),ejNil()))),_) => exCheck(ejHas(ejGet(S,"recordTypes"),N),"TYPE_NAME")
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Enum"),ejCons(ejString(N),ejNil()))),_) => exCheck(ejHas(ejGet(S,"enumTypes"),N),"TYPE_NAME")
  rule exResolveBase(S,ejArray(ejCons(ejString("Operation"),ejCons(ejString(N),ejNil()))),_) => exCheck(ejHas(ejGet(S,"operations"),N),"TYPE_NAME")
  rule exResolveBase(_,_,_) => "TYPE_NAME" [owise]
  rule exUnits(_,ejNil(),_,_) => ""
  rule exUnits(S,ejCons(ejArray(ejCons(ejString(N),ejCons(P,ejNil()))),L),Prev,C) => exFirst(exCheck(N >String Prev andBool exInt(P) =/=Int 0 andBool exInt(P) >=Int -128 andBool exInt(P) <=Int 127,C),exFirst(exCheck(exIn(ejString(N),ejGet(S,"units")),"TYPE_NAME"),exUnits(S,L,N,C)))
  rule exUnits(_,_,_,_) => "INPUT_SCHEMA" [owise]
  syntax Bool ::= exIn(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exIn(V,ejArray(L)) => ejMember(V,L)
  rule exIn(_,_) => false [owise]
endmodule

=== experiments/moriarty-language/formal/k/expression-v1.k sha256 92dd659e94f2d12df4ed22b7bedc531b6a2eda3b19971f25e9b4d0f3767c99bb ===
requires "expression-infer.k"
module MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-V1
  imports MORIARTY-EXPRESSION-INFER
  syntax EXResult ::= exPending() [symbol(exPending)] | exRejected(String,EJSON,EJList,Int) [symbol(exRejected)]
                    | exValue(EJSON,EJSON,Int) [symbol(exValue)] | exPrepared(EJSON,EJList,Int) [symbol(exPrepared)]
  configuration
    <k> $PGM:ERequest </k>
    <sigma> ejMissing() </sigma> <request> ejMissing() </request>
    <pre> ejMissing() </pre> <args> ejMissing() </args> <obs> ejMissing() </obs>
    <localTypes> ejObject(ejNil()) </localTypes> <locals> ejObject(ejNil()) </locals>
    <written> ejObject(ejNil()) </written> <writes> ejObject(ejNil()) </writes>
    <descriptors> ejNil() </descriptors> <sourceBytes> 0 </sourceBytes>
    <initialWork> 0 </initialWork> <work> 0 </work>
    <reducing> false </reducing> <out> exPending() </out>
  syntax KItem ::= exInput() | exSchemaPhase() | exShapePhase() | exTypePhase()
                 | exSnapshotPhase() | exBegin() | exFinish()
                 | exRequire(String,EJSON,EJList) | exCheckSite(EXSite)
                 | exStop(String,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exStaticStatements(EJList,Int,Bool)
                 | exStaticResult(EXInference,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exStaticBind(EXInference,String)
                 | exRunStatements(EJList,Int)
                 | exEnter(EJSON,EJList) | exReturned(EJSON,EJSON)
                 | exEvaluate(EJSON,EJList) | exApply(String,EJSON,EJSON,EJList,EJSON)
                 | exCollect(EJSON,EJList,EJList,EJList,Int)
                 | exCollectValue(EJSON,EJList,EJList,EJList,Int)
                 | exBooleanLeft(String,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exPublishExpression() | exDiscard()
  rule <k> expressionRequest(S,R) => exInput() </k> <sigma> _ => S </sigma> <request> _ => R </request>
  // Admission phases are separate continuations. No recursive snapshot traversal
  // is evaluated until the schema/cycle phase succeeds.
  rule <k> exRequire("",_,_) => .K ... </k>
  rule <k> exRequire(C,S,P) => exStop(C,S,P) ... </k> requires C =/=String ""
  rule <k> exStop(C,S,P) ~> _ => .K </k> <out> exPending() => exRejected(C,S,P,0) </out> <writes> _ => ejObject(ejNil()) </writes> <descriptors> _ => ejNil() </descriptors> <locals> _ => ejObject(ejNil()) </locals> <reducing> false </reducing>
  rule <k> exStop(C,S,P) ~> _ => .K </k> <out> exPending() => exRejected(C,S,P,I -Int W) </out> <writes> _ => ejObject(ejNil()) </writes> <descriptors> _ => ejNil() </descriptors> <locals> _ => ejObject(ejNil()) </locals> <reducing> true </reducing> <initialWork> I </initialWork> <work> W </work>
  rule <k> exInput() => exRequire(exCheck(exJsonBytes(S) <=Int 65536,"INPUT_BOUND"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exCheck(exJValid(S),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequestInput() ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma>
  syntax KItem ::= exRequestInput() | exRequestFields() | exWorkInput()
  rule <k> exRequestInput() => exRequire(exCheck(exJValid(R),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exCheck(ejClosed(R,exRequestKeys()),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequestFields() ... </k> <request> R </request>
  rule <k> exRequestFields() => exRequire(exCheck(ejGet(R,"contract") ==K ejString("moriarty-expression-contract/1") andBool ejIsString(ejGet(R,"source")),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exCheck(exUtf8(exText(ejGet(R,"source"))) <=Int 65536,"INPUT_BOUND"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exCheck(exDecimal(ejGet(R,"workInitial")),"INPUT_SCHEMA"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exWorkInput() ... </k> <request> R </request> <sourceBytes> _ => exUtf8(exText(ejGet(R,"source"))) </sourceBytes>
  rule <k> exWorkInput() => exRequire(exCheck(exInt(ejGet(R,"workInitial")) >=Int 0 andBool exInt(ejGet(R,"workInitial")) <=Int 65536,"INPUT_BOUND"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exCheck(exComponentsBound(S,R),"INPUT_BOUND"),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exSchemaPhase() ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <request> R </request> <initialWork> _ => exInt(ejGet(R,"workInitial")) </initialWork> <work> _ => exInt(ejGet(R,"workInitial")) </work>
  rule <k> exSchemaPhase() => exRequire(exSchemaShape(S),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exShapePhase() ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma>
  rule <k> exShapePhase() => exCheckSite(exCoreShape(ejGet(R,"core"),B)) ~> exRequire(exSchemaDomain(S),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exTypePhase() ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <request> R </request> <sourceBytes> B </sourceBytes>
  rule <k> exCheckSite(exGood()) => .K ... </k>
  rule <k> exCheckSite(exBad(C,S,P)) => exStop(C,S,P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exTypePhase() => exStaticStatements(exUnwrapArray(ejGet(ejGet(R,"core"),"statements")),0,false) ~> exSnapshotPhase() ... </k> <request> R </request> requires ejHas(ejGet(R,"core"),"statements")
  rule <k> exTypePhase() => exStaticResult(exInfer(ejGet(R,"core"),S,L,W,false,false,ejNil()),ejGet(R,"core"),ejNil()) ~> exSnapshotPhase() ... </k> <request> R </request> <sigma> S </sigma> <localTypes> L </localTypes> <written> W </written> requires notBool ejHas(ejGet(R,"core"),"statements")
  rule <k> exStaticStatements(ejNil(),_,_) => .K ... </k>
  rule <k> exStaticStatements(ejCons(N,Rest),I,E) => exRequire(exCheck(notBool E orBool ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("Ensure"),"TYPE_STATEMENT_PLACEMENT"),ejGet(N,"span"),ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())) ~> exStaticResult(exInfer(N,S,L,W,ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("Ensure"),true,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())),N,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())) ~> exStaticStatements(Rest,I +Int 1,E orBool ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("Ensure")) ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <localTypes> L </localTypes> <written> W </written>
  rule <k> exStaticResult(exTypeError(C,S,P),_,_) => exStop(C,S,P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exStaticResult(exTyped(_),N,P) => exStaticBind(exInfer(ejGet(ejGet(N,"operands"),"value"),S,L,W,false,false,ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString("0"),ejNil()))),exText(ejGet(ejGet(N,"operands"),"name"))) ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <localTypes> L </localTypes> <written> W </written> requires ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("Let")
  rule <k> exStaticBind(exTyped(T),N) => .K ... </k> <localTypes> L => ejPut(L,N,T) </localTypes>
  rule <k> exStaticBind(exTypeError(C,S,P),_) => exStop(C,S,P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exStaticResult(exTyped(_),N,_) => .K ... </k> <written> W => ejPut(W,exText(ejGet(ejGet(N,"operands"),"field")),ejBool(true)) </written> requires ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("NextWrite")
  rule <k> exStaticResult(exTyped(_),N,_) => .K ... </k> requires ejGet(N,"constructor") =/=K ejString("NextWrite") andBool ejGet(N,"constructor") =/=K ejString("Let")
  rule <k> exSnapshotPhase() => exRequire(exSnapshot(S,exFieldTypes(ejGet(S,"fields")),ejGet(R,"Pre")),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exSnapshot(S,ejGet(S,"args"),ejGet(R,"Args")),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exRequire(exSnapshot(S,ejGet(S,"observations"),ejGet(R,"Obs")),exSynthetic(),ejNil()) ~> exBegin() ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <request> R </request>
  rule <k> exBegin() => exLaunchCore(ejGet(R,"core")) ... </k> <request> R </request> <pre> _ => ejGet(R,"Pre") </pre> <args> _ => ejGet(R,"Args") </args> <obs> _ => ejGet(R,"Obs") </obs> <localTypes> _ => ejObject(ejNil()) </localTypes> <written> _ => ejObject(ejNil()) </written> <reducing> false => true </reducing>
  syntax KItem ::= exLaunchCore(EJSON)
  rule <k> exLaunchCore(N) => exRunStatements(exUnwrapArray(ejGet(N,"statements")),0) ~> exFinish() ... </k> requires ejHas(N,"statements")
  rule <k> exLaunchCore(N) => exEnter(N,ejNil()) ~> exPublishExpression() ... </k> requires notBool ejHas(N,"statements")
  rule <k> exEnter(N,P) => exStop("WORK_EXHAUSTED",ejGet(N,"span"),P) ... </k> <work> 0 </work>
  rule <k> exEnter(N,P) => exEvaluate(N,P) ... </k> <work> W => W -Int 1 </work> requires W >Int 0
  rule <k> exRunStatements(ejNil(),_) => .K ... </k>
  rule <k> exRunStatements(ejCons(N,L),I) => exEnter(N,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil())) ~> exDiscard() ~> exRunStatements(L,I +Int 1) ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturned(_,_) ~> exDiscard() => .K ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturned(T,V) ~> exPublishExpression() => .K </k> <work> W </work> <out> exPending() => exValue(T,V,W) </out>
  rule <k> exFinish() => exRequire(exSnapshotBounds(S,exFieldTypes(ejGet(S,"fields")),exOverlay(Pre,Writes),"VALUE_BOUND"),ejGet(ejGet(R,"core"),"span"),ejNil()) ~> exCommitCandidate() ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <request> R </request> <pre> Pre </pre> <writes> Writes </writes>
  syntax KItem ::= exCommitCandidate()
  rule <k> exCommitCandidate() => .K </k> <pre> Pre </pre> <writes> Writes </writes> <descriptors> D </descriptors> <work> W </work> <out> exPending() => exPrepared(exOverlay(Pre,Writes),D,W) </out>

  syntax Bool ::= exJValid(EJSON) [function, total]
                | exJListValid(EJList) [function, total]
  rule exJValid(ejString(_)) => true
  rule exJValid(ejBool(_)) => true
  rule exJValid(ejArray(L)) => exJListValid(L)
  rule exJValid(ejObject(L)) => exJListValid(L)
  rule exJValid(ejPair(_,V)) => exJValid(V)
  rule exJValid(_) => false [owise]
  rule exJListValid(ejNil()) => true
  rule exJListValid(ejCons(V,L)) => exJValid(V) andBool exJListValid(L)
  syntax Bool ::= exComponentsBound(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  rule exComponentsBound(S,R) => exJsonBytes(S) <=Int 65536 andBool exJsonBytes(ejGet(R,"core")) <=Int 65536 andBool exJsonBytes(ejGet(R,"Pre")) <=Int 65536 andBool exJsonBytes(ejGet(R,"Args")) <=Int 65536 andBool exJsonBytes(ejGet(R,"Obs")) <=Int 65536
  syntax EJList ::= exRequestKeys() [function]
  rule exRequestKeys() => ejCons(ejString("contract"),ejCons(ejString("source"),ejCons(ejString("core"),ejCons(ejString("Pre"),ejCons(ejString("Args"),ejCons(ejString("Obs"),ejCons(ejString("workInitial"),ejNil())))))))
  syntax EJSON ::= exFieldTypes(EJSON) [function, total]
                 | exOverlay(EJSON,EJSON) [function, total]
  syntax EJList ::= exFieldTypeList(EJList) [function, total]
  rule exFieldTypes(ejObject(L)) => ejObject(exFieldTypeList(L))
  rule exFieldTypes(_) => ejObject(ejNil()) [owise]
  rule exFieldTypeList(ejNil()) => ejNil()
  rule exFieldTypeList(ejCons(ejPair(N,F),L)) => ejCons(ejPair(N,ejGet(F,"type")),exFieldTypeList(L))
  rule exOverlay(P,ejObject(ejNil())) => P
  rule exOverlay(P,ejObject(ejCons(ejPair(N,V),L))) => exOverlay(ejPut(P,N,V),ejObject(L))
  rule exOverlay(P,_) => P [owise]
  // Entry is charged once. Child frames retain original paths and spans.
  rule <k> exEvaluate(N,P) => exEnter(ejGet(ejGet(N,"operands"),"left"),ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString("0"),ejNil()))) ~> exBooleanLeft(exText(ejGet(N,"constructor")),N,P) ... </k> requires ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("And") orBool ejGet(N,"constructor") ==K ejString("Or")
  rule <k> exReturned(_,ejBool(false)) ~> exBooleanLeft("And",_,_) => exReturned(exType("Bool"),ejBool(false)) ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturned(_,ejBool(true)) ~> exBooleanLeft("Or",_,_) => exReturned(exType("Bool"),ejBool(true)) ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturned(_,ejBool(true)) ~> exBooleanLeft("And",N,P) => exEnter(ejGet(ejGet(N,"operands"),"right"),ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString("1"),ejNil()))) ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturned(_,ejBool(false)) ~> exBooleanLeft("Or",N,P) => exEnter(ejGet(ejGet(N,"operands"),"right"),ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString("1"),ejNil()))) ... </k>
  rule <k> exEvaluate(N,P) => exCollect(N,P,exChildren(exText(ejGet(N,"constructor")),ejGet(N,"operands")),ejNil(),0) ... </k> requires ejGet(N,"constructor") =/=K ejString("And") andBool ejGet(N,"constructor") =/=K ejString("Or")
  rule <k> exCollect(N,P,ejCons(C,R),V,I) => exEnter(C,ejAppend(P,ejCons(ejString(Int2String(I)),ejNil()))) ~> exCollectValue(N,P,R,V,I) ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturned(_,V) ~> exCollectValue(N,P,R,Vs,I) => exCollect(N,P,R,ejAppend(Vs,ejCons(V,ejNil())),I +Int 1) ... </k>
  rule <k> exCollect(N,P,ejNil(),V,_) => exApplyTyped(exInfer(N,S,L,ejObject(ejNil()),true,exStatement(exText(ejGet(N,"constructor"))),P),N,P,ejArray(V)) ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma> <localTypes> L </localTypes>
  syntax KItem ::= exApplyTyped(EXInference,EJSON,EJList,EJSON)
                 | exReturnChecked(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJList)
  rule <k> exApplyTyped(exTyped(T),N,P,V) => exApply(exText(ejGet(N,"constructor")),ejGet(N,"operands"),T,P,ejArray(ejCons(ejGet(N,"span"),ejCons(V,ejNil())))) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApplyTyped(exTypeError(C,Span,P),_,_,_) => exStop(C,Span,P) ... </k>
  // Payload packages span and operand values; it carries no host-generated verdict.
  rule <k> exApply(K,O,T,P,Payload) => exReturnChecked(T,exLiteralValue(K,O),ejAt(Payload,0),P) ... </k> requires exLiteral(K)
  rule <k> exApply("ReadLocal",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,exGetName(L,ejGet(O,"name")),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> <locals> L </locals>
  rule <k> exApply("ReadArg",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,exGetName(A,ejGet(O,"name")),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> <args> A </args>
  rule <k> exApply("ReadObs",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,exGetName(A,ejGet(O,"name")),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> <obs> A </obs>
  rule <k> exApply("ReadPre",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,exGetName(Pre,ejGet(O,"field")),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> <pre> Pre </pre> requires ejGet(O,"view") ==K ejString("pre")
  rule <k> exApply("ReadPre",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,exGetName(exOverlay(Pre,W),ejGet(O,"field")),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> <pre> Pre </pre> <writes> W </writes> requires ejGet(O,"view") ==K ejString("post")
  rule <k> exApply(K,O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,exGetName(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0),ejGet(O,"field")),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> requires K ==String "ProjectField" orBool K ==String "AccessField"
  rule <k> exApply(K,_,T,P,X) => exRequire(exCheck(exInt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),1)) <Int exArrayLength(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0)),"INDEX_RANGE"),ejAt(X,0),P) ~> exReturnChecked(T,ejAt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0),exInt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),1))),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> requires K ==String "ProjectIndex" orBool K ==String "AccessIndex"
  rule <k> exApply("ConstructRecord",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejObject(exSortPairs(exZipFields(exUnwrapArray(ejGet(O,"fields")),exUnwrapArray(ejAt(X,1))))),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply("ConstructEnum",O,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejGet(O,"member"),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply("ConstructNone",_,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejArray(ejNil()),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply("ConstructSome",_,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejArray(ejCons(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0),ejNil())),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply("ConstructCollection",_,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejAt(X,1),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply(K,_,T,P,X) => exArithmetic(K,T,exInt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0)),exInt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),1)),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> requires K ==String "Add" orBool K ==String "Sub" orBool K ==String "Mul" orBool K ==String "FloorDiv" orBool K ==String "CeilDiv"
  rule <k> exApply("Eq",_,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejBool(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0) ==K ejAt(ejAt(X,1),1)),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply(K,_,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejBool(exCompare(K,exInt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0)),exInt(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),1)))),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k> requires K ==String "Lt" orBool K ==String "Lte" orBool K ==String "Gt" orBool K ==String "Gte"
  rule <k> exApply("Not",_,T,P,X) => exReturnChecked(T,ejBool(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0) ==K ejBool(false)),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exApply(K,_,T,P,X) => exRequire(exCheck(ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0) ==K ejBool(true),exGuardCode(K)),ejAt(X,0),P) ~> exReturned(T,ejMissing()) ... </k> requires K ==String "Require" orBool K ==String "Ensure"
  rule <k> exApply("Let",O,T,_,X) => exReturned(T,ejMissing()) ... </k> <locals> L => ejPut(L,exText(ejGet(O,"name")),ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0)) </locals> <localTypes> LT => ejPut(LT,exText(ejGet(O,"name")),exInferredType(exInfer(ejGet(O,"value"),S,LT,ejObject(ejNil()),false,false,ejNil()))) </localTypes> <sigma> S </sigma>
  rule <k> exApply("NextWrite",O,T,_,X) => exReturned(T,ejMissing()) ... </k> <writes> W => ejPut(W,exText(ejGet(O,"field")),ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0)) </writes>
  rule <k> exApply("Emit",O,T,P,X) => exEmitCheck(T,ejObject(ejCons(ejPair("fields",ejAt(ejAt(X,1),0)),ejCons(ejPair("operation",ejGet(O,"operation")),ejNil()))),ejAt(X,0),P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exReturnChecked(T,V,Span,P) => exRequire(exValueBound(S,T,V,"VALUE_BOUND"),Span,P) ~> exReturned(T,V) ... </k> <sigma> S </sigma>
  syntax KItem ::= exArithmetic(String,EJSON,Int,Int,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exArithmeticResult(EJSON,Int,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exDivide(String,EJSON,Int,Int,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exEmitCheck(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJList)
                 | exEmitAppend(EJSON,EJSON,EJSON,EJList)
  rule <k> exArithmetic("Add",T,A,B,S,P) => exArithmeticResult(T,A +Int B,S,P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exArithmetic("Sub",T,A,B,S,P) => exArithmeticResult(T,A -Int B,S,P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exArithmetic("Mul",T,A,B,S,P) => exArithmeticResult(T,A *Int B,S,P) ... </k>
  rule <k> exArithmetic(K,T,A,B,S,P) => exRequire(exCheck(B >Int 0,"ARITH_DENOMINATOR"),S,P) ~> exDivide(K,T,A,B,S,P) ... </k> requires K ==String "FloorDiv" orBool K ==String "CeilDiv"
  rule <k> exDivide("FloorDiv",T,A,B,S,P) => exArithmeticResult(T,exFloor(A,B),S,P) ... </k> requires B >Int 0
  rule <k> exDivide("CeilDiv",T,A,B,S,P) => exArithmeticResult(T,0 -Int exFloor(0 -Int A,B),S,P) ... </k> requires B >Int 0
  rule <k> exArithmeticResult(T,N,S,P) => exRequire(exCheck(exFits(T,ejString(Int2String(N))),"ARITH_RANGE"),S,P) ~> exReturnChecked(T,ejString(Int2String(N)),S,P) ... </k>
  syntax Int ::= exFloor(Int,Int) [function]
  rule exFloor(A,B) => A /Int B requires B >Int 0 andBool A >=Int 0
  rule exFloor(A,B) => (0 -Int ((0 -Int A) /Int B)) -Int 1 requires B >Int 0 andBool A <Int 0 andBool (0 -Int A) %Int B =/=Int 0
  rule exFloor(A,B) => 0 -Int ((0 -Int A) /Int B) requires B >Int 0 andBool A <Int 0 andBool (0 -Int A) %Int B ==Int 0
  syntax Bool ::= exCompare(String,Int,Int) [function, total]
  rule exCompare("Lt",A,B) => A <Int B
  rule exCompare("Lte",A,B) => A <=Int B
  rule exCompare("Gt",A,B) => A >Int B
  rule exCompare("Gte",A,B) => A >=Int B
  rule exCompare(_,_,_) => false [owise]
  syntax String ::= exGuardCode(String) [function, total]
  rule exGuardCode("Require") => "GUARD_FAILED"
